The registry now returns a separate prospective setup offer. Its candidates price the complete declared draft and optional forecast before download, preserve explicit environment overrides, and identify the destination volume through the nearest existing ancestor when the model directory does not yet exist. The shared pure planner remains the authority for feasibility. The offer cannot be passed as an activation decision or an installed acceptance record. Actual Auto activation still considers only accepted installed manifests, reobserves the actual directory and components, authenticates payloads and checks fresh load admission. Explicit unavailable choices are refused rather than replaced. The compiled supported registry still contains only the original pack and no qualified Auto profile.

LocalInference now supplies setup from the selected offer, bound to the quantization, active memory ceiling and independent live-memory policy that the user reviewed. The collection root is resolved once at owner creation, so a later default-model locator change cannot redirect another pack. Existing custom original directories remain original-only. Prospective setup can credit this owner's loaded physical footprint once because setup unloads it; this credit is not used for load admission. Merely reviewing an offer opens no acceptance journal, creates no model directory, downloads nothing and starts no model. Read-only offers may reflect queued settings while existing work drains, whereas acceptance remains under exclusive model maintenance.

The Mac setup panel receives the selected pack instead of always constructing an original-pack setup. It shows the quantization, recommendation reason and complete installation bytes, including optional components. Requests have revision and preference checks so a delayed result cannot overwrite newer settings. A completed verification is retained for unchanged setup choices. A changed active ceiling, quantization or live policy invalidates the earlier offer; readiness and an unused custom value do not. If preferences change during download, the runtime refuses the stale acceptance before reaching the inference owner, keeps the files, and refreshes setup for the new choice. File verification is explicitly labeled separately from the later load and startup check.

Prepared native fixtures cover prospective component pricing, forecast overrides, the separation between offers and installed eligibility, unavailable choices, duplicate observations, storage ancestry without directory creation, inactive versus active setting edits, original-directory ownership and refusal of stale setup before acceptance. Thirty-two static entry checks pass locally and the diff check is clean. No local compilation, tensor scan, model process or rendered-view acceptance ran for this stage. The native fixtures and the changed UI still require CI and visual review, followed by actual setup/activation acceptance when the serialized quality study completes.

The prior durable-acceptance source passes the instrumented catalogue with 104 checks, zero failures or skips and 40,876 assertions, including the exact historical startup recipe comparison. Its public-library and Xcode jobs pass. The captured full engine and Mac workflows still have weights-free and scripted checks running. These receipts do not certify this setup-offer stage. The held-out quality continuation is running job twenty-seven, with twenty-seven completed jobs and no aggregate outcome inspected. Final quality and speed campaigns, standalone production and independent public pull, actual loaded-profile confirmation, qualified alternative registration and integrated release remain open. No installed model, CLI, public pack or release changes.
